Document 001: 10年期美债收益率创24年新高，Amundi、Pimco逆势加仓：实际回报近3%

Source PDF: all report pdf/10年期美债收益率创24年新高，Amundi、Pimco逆势加仓：实际回报近3%.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

美债剧烈抛售推⾼ 10 年期收益率⾄ 5.36% 的 24 年⾼位，但⾼⾼达 3% 的实际回报率触发了顶
级机构的逆势布局。 Amundi 与 Pimco 等资产管理巨头正趁估值回调及股债⽐价逆转之际加
⼤美债配置，三季度美债基⾦净流⼊创新⾼。
摘要：
美债遭遇持续抛售、 10 年期收益率触及 24 年峰值之际，全球顶级资产管理机构正反向布局，将
这轮史罕⻅的⾼收益率视为锁定⻓期固定回报的历史性窗⼝。
Amundi ⾸席投资官 Vincent Mortier 表⽰，这家管理规模达 2.6 万亿欧元的资产管理巨头正在 " 逐
步增加 " 美元计价投资组合对美债的敞⼝。Pimco ⾸席投资官 Dan Ivascyn 则指出，尽管美债价格
可能继续下跌，但与处于历史⾼位附近的股票相⽐，美债估值更具吸引⼒。市场数据显⽰，以
通胀预期折算， 10 年期美债⽬前提供的实际回报率接近 3% ，这⼀⽔平正在吸引⼤量机构资⾦⼊
场。
资⾦流向数据印证了上述机构的⾏动。据彭博根据 EPFR 数据的测算，今年第三季度美国政府债

券基⾦净流⼊资⾦达到创纪录的 593 亿美元， 10 ⽉以来更已追加流⼊近 140 亿美元。
收益率冲顶，触发逢⾼买⼊
美债市场今年以来经历了⼀轮罕⻅的剧烈抛售。 10 年期美债收益率年内累计上⾏逾 106 个基点，
上周三在拍卖前数⼩时攀升⾄ 5.36% ，创下 24 年来新⾼。此轮抛售由多重因素驱动，包括能源
成本因特朗普政府对伊朗战争政策⽽上升、⼈⼯智能公司⼤规模发债以及市场对美国经济持续
强劲增⻓的预期。
然⽽，正是这⼀⾼收益率⽔平触发了机构的逆势布局。 Mortier 指出，市场对未来⼗年平均通胀
率约 2.35% 的预期，意味着当前 10 年期美债可提供接近 3% 的实际回报， " 这⼀⽔平⾃然会吸引新
的兴趣 " 。
从拍卖结果来看，机构需求已现端倪。上周三 390 亿美元 10 年期国债拍卖需求强劲，⼀级交易商
—— 即负责吸纳未被投资者认购部分的⼤型银⾏ —— 认购⽐例降⾄ 2003 年以来最低，显⽰市场
投资者直接认购意愿显著增强。上周的 3 年期和 30 年期国债拍卖同样表现稳健。

相对价值凸显，股债⽐较优势逆转
机构加仓美债的逻辑，不仅来⾃绝对收益的吸引⼒，更源于股债相对价值的重新评估。
Pimco 的 Dan Ivascyn 表⽰，当前股票估值⾼度拉伸、信⽤利差极度收窄，企业债相对于超低⻛
险的美国国债所提供的额外补偿极为有限， " 当你审视股票估值有多紧绷、信⽤利差有多压缩，
我们认为这些利率⽔平更具吸引⼒。 "
PGIM Credit 联席⾸席投资官 Greg Peters 态度同样明确： " 美国 10 年期收益率站上 5% ，是⼀个不
错的中⻓期持有标的，是相当好的投资。 "
Morgan Stanley Investment Management 宽市场固定收益主管 Vishal Khanduja 则认为，当前收
益率⽔平 " 应当⾜够吸引投资者将资⾦从现⾦、浮动利率证券或其他配置中转移出来，锁定固定
利率回报 " 。

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

期限选择分歧，⻓端⻛险仍受争议
尽管对美债整体前景看法趋于积极，机构投资者在期限选择上仍存在明显分歧，⻓端美债⾯临
的供需技术⾯压⼒令部分投资者保持谨慎。
Peters 直⾔： " 我不确定我会配置 30 年期美债。 "Khanduja 将 10 年期以内品种标记为 " 有吸引⼒ " ，
但表⽰对 20 年和 30 年期的 " 确信度较低 " 。他指出，期限越⻓，供需技术⾯的⼲扰越剧烈，并特别
提及财政部⻓⻉森特扩⼤美国国债回购规模的决定 ——" 当⾮经济性参与者介⼊并扭曲该期限段
的供需技术⾯时，很难建⽴清晰的估值逻辑。 "
对于急于 " 抄底 " 但可能买在半⼭腰的⻛险， Khanduja 以数学逻辑作出回应：
展望更⼴泛的资⾦流向， Peters 还指出欧元区局势可能为美债带来额外的资⾦流⼊。法国主权债

务在明年春季⼤选临近之际遭遇⼤幅抛售。 " 我完全可以预⻅，⼀旦法国出现问题，欧元区收益
率进⼀步联动攀升，资⾦将更多地流⼊美国国债市场。 " 他说。
" 收益所提供的缓冲已经⾜够充裕，在这些价位持有⽐等待收益率的终极顶部或底部更为理性。 "

利率上行引發波動，惟無損 AI 供應鏈擴張動能
2026 年 10 月
9 月全球股市普遍回落，主因為油價急升、全球長端殖利率攀高及主要央行轉鷹。僅有日本股市
受 AI、半導體需求與企業信心支撐，抵銷 BOJ 升息壓力，成為主要市場中唯一正報酬。標普 500 9
月小跌 0.3%，雖 AI 與大型科技股獲利韌性仍在，但 Fed 於 9 月升息並暗示年底前再度升息，加上
油價與美債殖利率走高，壓抑估值並引發科技股震盪。印度股市下跌 6.9%最弱，主要受油價飆升、
全球利率上行及外資單月淨流出約 27 億美元拖累。中國股市下跌 4.7%，因受內需修復偏弱、科技

股評價承壓所致；歐洲股市也因油價單月大漲近 14%與全球殖利率攀升影響，9 月下跌 4.6%。
9 月主要債市全面下跌，核心壓力來自全球殖利率急升。美歐日央行同步升息、Brent 原油單月
上漲約 14%、 美國經濟數據偏強與公債供給疑慮 ， 使美國10 年期殖利率單月上升約 53bp，創 2022
年以來最大升幅。主要券種中，全球投資級公司債下跌 2.93%表現最弱，新興美元債亦下跌 2.91%，
因 9 月全球公債殖利率快速攀升，此兩類債券對利率變化均較敏感 ； 其中投資級公司債相較高收益債
存續期較長、票息緩衝較低，因此在利率重新定價期間承受較大價格壓力。亞洲高收益債下挫 1.20%
相對抗跌，因高票息與較短存續期提供緩衝；新興當地債跌 1.74%，亦優於多數美元債種。
在股市方面，AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器 DRAM、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供
應鏈維持成長動能，市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分化。若AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資
本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整

體而言 ， 科技產業與台灣及日本股市獲利表現仍具關注度 ， 惟後續仍須留意全球景氣 、 產業供需變化 、
政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
就債市而言，雖然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶
來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率
環境下，中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級
公司債指數利差約 80 個基點 ， 雖然利差偏窄 ， 但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準 。 因此，
在目前市場環境下 ， 中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別 ， 惟後續表現仍須
留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

美國股市：通膨與升息預期上升推動美債利率上漲，美股呈高檔區間震盪
9 月因美伊關係緊張升溫 ， 推升油價並加劇市場對通膨回升的擔憂 ；相較於 6 月點陣圖 ，9 月FOMC
官員全面上修 2026 至 2027 年的政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示 2026 年底前會再升息一次，加上美
國經濟數據表現優於預期，使美國公債殖利率走升，進而壓抑美股表現。在此背景下，9 月標普 500
指數呈現高檔區間震盪格局。產業表現方面，資訊科技與通訊服務類股相對強勢，持續扮演指數上漲
的主要動能；相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費及部分工業類股表現較為疲弱。

9 月美國聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 的表決結果 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調25 個基點至
3.75%到 4.00%。9 月最新經濟預測，美國聯準會官員上修 2026 年核心 PCE 至 3.4%，2027 年維持
在 2.5%，將 2026-2027 年經濟成長上調 0.1%至 2.3%與 2.4%，顯示聯準會認為美國經濟與通膨仍
具韌性。
觀察美國近期的經濟數據可以發現企業投資動能依然強健，元大投顧表示，美國8 月扣除飛機與
國防的核心資本財訂單由前期的月增 0.6%加速至 1.6%， 其中初級金屬 、 機械及電機設備訂單均呈現
增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成長形
成穩健貢獻。
自 6 月底以來 ， 市場持續上修標普500 指數企業獲利預估 ， 其中以科技與能源類股貢獻最為明顯 ，
反映企業營運表現優於市場預期。AI 投資循環持續推進、企業數位轉型需求增加，以及美國內需維
持穩健，共同支撐企業獲利成長動能，並有助於吸收高利率環境帶來的壓力。與此同時，標普 500

指數前瞻本益比已由先前高點回落，評價壓力較先前明顯緩解。隨著企業獲利持續改善，美股行情正
由過去仰賴資金與情緒推升評價 ， 逐步轉向由基本面支撐股價 ， 後續表現將更取決於企業獲利是否持
續優於預期。
投資策略方面，投資焦點已逐漸由評價擴張轉向企業獲利成長與基本面表現。短期市場仍須留意
政策面不確定性 ， 美中貿易談判進展將牽動全球供應鏈與科技產業展望 ， 若關稅或科技限制措施再度
升溫，可能對科技股評價造成壓力；此外，美國期中選舉逐漸接近，企業稅率、科技監管及產業政策
等議題亦可能影響市場風險偏好。整體而言，企業獲利改善仍是支撐美股最重要的力量，若 AI 投資
循環與獲利成長趨勢延續，美股中長期表現仍可望由基本面驅動。

歐洲股市：儘管企業獲利上修，仍需持續關注地緣政治發展
9 月泛歐 600 指數下跌約 2%，產業表現方面 ，製藥 、生技、銀行及部分科技類股表現相對強勢，
顯示資金偏好具獲利能見度與防禦特性的族群 ； 相較之下 ， 礦業 、 消費及部分金融服務類股表現落後 ，
反映市場對歐洲經濟成長及企業獲利前景仍持審慎態度。整體而言，受到高油價以及高利率環境，投
資氣氛轉趨保守，市場資金持續聚焦於基本面較穩健的產業。
歐洲央行 9 月政策利率由 2.25% 升息 1 碼至 2.50 % ，符合市場預期。歐洲央行最新經濟預
測中上修中期通膨展望，2027 年消費者物價指數年增率由 2.3% 升至 2.5% ，2028 年則由 2.0%

上調至 2.1% ；核心消費者物價指數方面，2027 年和 2028 年預測亦各上修 0.1 % 至 2.6% 及
2.3% ，能源成本上升對食品及其他價格的傳導，使得通膨回落速度較原先預期緩慢。ECB 總裁拉加
德表示能源價格上漲逐步傳導至核心及食品通膨，預期整體通膨在 2027 年上半年維持在高檔。
根據花旗歐洲獲利修正指數(Earnings Revision Index) ，歐洲企業獲利預估已連續上修並到近五
年高點，反映企業基本面持續改善。受惠於歐元區景氣穩步復甦、製造業景氣回升，以及企業資本支
出逐步回暖，工業、金融及景氣循環類股獲利展望同步改善。隨著市場對企業未來盈餘預期提高，有
助支撐歐股評價面與資金流入，並成為推動泛歐 600 指數表現的重要動能。
然而，自 3 月美伊衝突爆發以來，市場對能源供給中斷的擔憂持續升溫，帶動油價走高，進而增
加仰賴進口能源的歐洲企業成本壓力，並壓抑歐股相對表現。進入9 月後，俄羅斯與北約緊張關係再
度升溫，歐洲安全局勢不確定性提高，進一步加劇市場對能源供應與地緣政治風險的擔憂。若相關衝

突持續延燒，不僅可能推升能源價格與通膨壓力，也可能影響企業投資意願及市場風險偏好，進而對
歐洲企業獲利與股市表現形成壓力。
展望 10 月，歐股仍將受到高油價、地緣政治風險及歐洲央行維持偏鷹立場等因素影響，市場波
動性可能維持偏高。近期能源價格攀升推升通膨預期，使 ECB 傾向維持較高利率水準，不僅提高企
業融資成本，也限制股市評價進一步擴張的空間。此外，中東局勢持續緊張，以及俄羅斯與北約間的
緊張關係再度升高，均可能影響投資人風險偏好，增添歐洲金融市場不確定性。雖然歐元區經濟活動
仍維持溫和擴張，企業獲利預估亦持續改善，但在高利率與地緣政治風險尚未明顯緩解之前，市場情
緒仍偏向審慎，後續仍需持續關注能源價格、通膨走勢及地緣政治發展，作為判斷歐股能否重啟上漲
動能的重要觀察指標。

日本股市：升息落地，景氣展望佳，第四季半導體與商社企業獲利動能有望延續
9 月日本股市受升息預期及長天期公債殖利率走高影響，部分高評價股票出現震盪，但這波調整
較偏向政策轉折下的評價重估，而非企業基本面全面轉弱。日本銀行於 9 月 18 日決議將短期政策利
率目標提高至約 1.25%，使市場反覆討論的升息方向逐漸明朗。隨著政策落地，投資焦點有望由利率
變動轉回企業營運與獲利。從正面角度看，利率正常化反映日本經濟具備一定韌性，股價整理也有助
於消化先前累積的評價壓力。若後續企業財測保持穩健，9 月的震盪可望成為第四季行情重新蓄勢的

過程。
日本股市的中期支撐，來自薪資成長、內需改善與企業投資的相互帶動。2026 年春鬥最終統計
顯示，5,368 個工會的平均調薪幅度達 5.01%，連續第三年維持在 5%以上；可明確辨識的基本薪資
調升幅度亦達 3.50%。薪資持續增加，有助於提升家庭消費能力，為零售與服務等內需產業提供較穩
定的需求基礎。若企業能透過產品競爭力、定價能力及經營效率吸收成本，消費回溫便有機會轉化為
獲利成長。同時，全球 AI 相關投資也為日本半導體設備、載板及電子零組件供應鏈創造機會，使內
需復甦與科技需求形成雙重支撐。
展望 10 月，隨著升息議題逐步由預期轉為已知條件，市場可望更加重視第四季訂單、企業財測
及實際獲利。科技產業方面，若全球 AI 相關資本支出持續擴大，日本具技術優勢的設備與零組件企
業，有機會將需求轉化為接單及出貨成長；商社與資源相關企業，則可觀察能源、原物料業務及投資
收益對現金流的貢獻。內需產業亦值得留意薪資成長能否帶動消費，以及企業能否維持利潤率。整體
而言，10 月的投資機會不必侷限於單一題材，市場有望逐步從政策消息主導，轉向由產業需求與企

業業績支撐的行情。
投資策略方面，半導體供應鏈可優先挑選訂單能見度高、具技術門檻且獲利成長明確的企業；商
社則著重現金流、資本配置與股東回饋，內需股則觀察消費回溫能否反映在營收及利潤。若股價因利
率或匯率波動而短暫回落，但企業基本面與財測未明顯改變，可考慮分批布局，避免追逐短線漲幅。
後續仍須追蹤長天期利率 、 日圓及企業財測變化 ， 但投資主軸可更積極地放在具備實質成長能力的公
司。日本央行升息落地後，企業獲利能否接棒，將是日本股市延續表現的關鍵。

台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產 ， 則為自動化設備 、 銅箔及高階材料創造機會 。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨 、 自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進 ， 相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件 。 與此同時 ， 低價及開放權重策略持續降低AI 使用門檻 ， 帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月 ， 中國AI 的成長空間仍在 ， 但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模 ， 逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善 。 晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要 ， 價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用 ， 讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值 。 光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸 ， 也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展 ， 拓展供應商的產品空間 。Lumentum 指出 ，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升 ， 帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長 。 高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進 。 雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。 「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求 。 配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心 ， 再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程 ， 入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠 ； 選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理 。 一方面 ， 拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期 ，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力 ， 比市場原先擔心的情況更好 。 其中一個重要原因是 ， 新興市場政府透過燃油補貼 、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變 ， 並將2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%， 低於6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點 ，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策 ， 為巴西央行創造更多降息空間 ， 有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美 各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外 ，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響 ，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶 ，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外 ，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復 ，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升 ， 帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油 ， 轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運 ， 帶動拉斯坦努拉港9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修 ， 且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升 ，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點 ， 加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金ETF 需
求下滑 ， 高盛因此將2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元 。 然而 ， 高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪 。 然而 ， 全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金 ， 加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置 ，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響 ， 美國經濟仍維持強勁成長 ，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮 ，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量 ， 投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素 ，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降 ， 存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響 。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債 。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 歐洲央行上周四(9/10)為年內第二次上調利率，以應對能源價格上漲帶來的通膨
壓力 ， 並警告物價壓力可能比先前預期更久 ， 推動市場加大對後續進一步收緊政
策的押注 。 歐洲央行總裁拉加德形容此次决定爲”無需多想的决定”， 並警告通膨
回落至 2%目標的時間點目前預計在 2027 年底。歐洲央行預期明年平均通膨爲
2.5%，高於 6 月的 2.3%；2027 年核心通膨預期爲 2.6%，高於此前的 2.5%。
⚫ 軍事消息人士稱，與伊朗結盟的胡塞武裝上周四(9/10)奪取葉門港口城市的控制
權 ， 並沿紅海海岸向南推進至戰略島嶼地區 。 美國總統川普表示 ， 他預計伊朗戰
爭將在美國中期選舉後結束。若胡塞武裝控制這條與 荷姆茲海峽同樣重要的水
道 ， 其支持者伊朗將在與美國的戰爭中獲得關鍵戰略優勢 ， 並進一步推高油價 。
⚫ 美國勞工部勞工統計局上週四(9/10)公布 8 月生産者物價指數 PPI 年增 5.4%，

高於市場預期的 5.3％。週五(9/11)公布 8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 3.4%與前
月相同，核心 CPI 年增率 2.4%符合市場預期，月增率達 0.3%高於市場預期。
FED WATCH顯示數據公布後推升市場對於聯準會本周將升息的預期。
⚫ 日本央行數據顯示，8 月國內企業物價指數較去年同期上漲 7.6%。增幅高於市
場預估中值 7.4%。7 月漲幅經修正後爲 7.7%；8 月進口物價指數同比上漲 24.8%，
7 月修正後爲上漲 29.3%， 表明當月日圓貶值以及中東局勢影響推高進口成本和
整體通膨，同時鞏固市場對日本央行在 9 月會議進一步升息預期。

美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Sep/14/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.455，低點在 31.698，以 31.638 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 0.8 分。
上週台幣先升後貶，週初，受到市場對於日本央行 9 月升息的預期升溫，日圓
走升帶動美元指數跌破 99 整數價位低檔震盪 ， 加以台股高檔震盪 ， 外資延續匯入的
走勢，帶動台幣升破31.500 至 31.455 週內高點；臨近週末，美國公布8 月生產者物
價指數年增率由上個月的 4.8%上升至 5.4%，數據公布後，FEDWATCH顯示聯準會
9 月升息機率攀升至 7 成，美元指數回到 99 上方，台股高點回落，外資偏向匯出，
台幣由升轉貶至 31.698 週內低點，終場在央行調節之下收在 31.638。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣區間震盪。國際市場方面，上週五公布美國 8 月核心消費者物價
指數月增率 0.3%高於市場預期的 0.2%，數據公布後，提高本週聯準會升息的機率

至接近 9 成，此外中東局勢仍有升溫風險，油價持續高檔震盪，美元指數在 99 整數
關口盤整；就台灣市場來看，台灣 8 月出口衝上 824 億美元，創下歷年單月新高紀
錄，月增 9.4%，年增 41%，展現極為強勁的成長動能。顯示受惠於 AI 需求全面爆
發，帶動外銷出口暢旺，基本面強勁持續支撐台幣的表現；盤勢上，匯價逢低吸引
進口商及政府基金買盤進場，逢高則面臨出口商賣美金，且本週台美均有利率決策
會議，預估匯率波動將加劇。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間震盪。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.400-31.900

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/14/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週人民幣震盪走升。週初，雖然美伊戰火未平，帶動油價及美債利率高漲，
但市場對於日本央行 9 月升息預期升溫，日圓偏升帶動美元指數跌破 99 價位後低
檔震盪，人民幣延續偏升走勢，走升至6.7022 週內高點；臨近週末，美國公布8 月
生產者物價指數 5.4%優於市場預期 5.3%， 加以8 月核心消費者物價指數月增率 0.3%
優於市場預期的 0.2%，數據公布後，FEDWATCH顯示本週聯準會 9 月升息預期大
幅攀升至近 9 成，美元指數回到99 上方，人民幣回吐部分週初以來的漲幅，終場境
外人民幣收在 6.7071，週線升值 8 點，境內人民幣則收在6.7079，週線升值 29 點。  
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間偏升。國際市場方面，美國通膨數據表現優於市場預期，
加以油價攀升至每桶 100 美元上方 ， 美債殖利率位居高檔 ， 美元指數也在99 上方盤
整，關注本週聯準會最新利率決議，預計在會議結果出爐以前，聯準會升息預期將
支撐美元指數，限制人民幣升值的空間。中國國內方面，人行中間價格緩步偏升，
上週升破 6.7800 價位，持續釋放人民幣偏強信號，支撐人民幣延續升值的方向。技
術面方面 ，USD/CNH 匯價居於各天期均線下方 ，且均線向下發散 ，加以匯價由低點
向上反彈測試 6.7150 關鍵價位後面臨壓力，顯示空方動能較盛，預計人民幣將延續
升值的態勢。綜合以上，預估本週人民幣走勢區間偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6800-6.7300

美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/14/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌 。週初 ，因市場預期日本央行將加快升息步伐及資金將
回流日本，使 USD/JPY 跌破至 155 整數關卡之下；週間，USD/JPY 延續跌勢最低
來到 152.87 後反彈，後續大致在 153 至 154 間波動；臨近週末，美國公布 8 月 PPI
及 CPI 數據顯示通膨仍處高位 ，USD/JPY 一度反彈至154.6 後回落 ， 終場收在153.54，
週線大跌 270 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、上週公布日本第二季 GDP
較上季增長 0.4%，季增年率也由 1.1%上升至 1.4%，主因為企業資本支出改善，而
7 月名目薪資年增 4.7%為 30 年來最大漲幅，而實質薪資年增 2.4%也為 5 年多來最
大漲幅，顯示日本經濟復甦力道較原先預期強勁，進一步強化市場對於日央升息的
預期，有利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月 CPI 及 PPI 數據顯示美國通膨仍處高位，強化
市場對聯準會升息預期，且中東局勢升溫，帶動油價突破 100 美元，預期將支撐美
元，限縮 USD/JPY 下行空間。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價多次向
下測試 153 整數關卡未能有效突破，顯示下方有買盤支撐，而 155 關卡附近則為前
波壓力位，因此短線先以區間看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的
可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：152.00-157.00

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 小幅下跌。 週初 ，EUR/USD 大致在 1.1600 至 1.1635 窄幅波動；
週間，市場等待歐洲央行利率決議及 美國通膨數據，EUR/USD 持續在 1.1615 至
1.1653 波動；週四晚間公布歐洲央行利率決議如預期升息 1 碼，而美國 8 月 PPI 高
於預期使市場對聯準會本月升息預期上升，EUR/USD 下行跌破 1.16 整數關口；臨
近週末，美國公布 8 月 CPI 顯示通膨仍具韌性，帶動 EUR/USD 走低來至 1.1568，
終場收在 1.1598，週線下跌 15 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、上週歐洲央行公布利率決
議如預期升息一碼，總裁拉加德表示即使面對能源價格衝擊，歐洲經濟第二季依然
展現韌性，預期第三季仍會延續，但未來通膨仍面臨上行風險，偏鷹派言論使市場
對於歐洲央行未來持續升息的預期上升，將帶給歐元支撐。二、美國 8 月 PPI 月增
0.4%符合市場預期 ， 年增5.4%高於預期 ，而 8 月 CPI 月增 0.4%年增 3.4%符合預期 ，
顯示通膨仍維持高位，使市場對聯準會升息預期增加，將支撐美元，不利歐元匯價
上行。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖一度突破 1.1630 年線但未
能站穩，顯示上方仍有賣壓，而下方1.1550 附近則有支撐，在有效突破前以區間震
盪看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1530-1.1680

息機率升至七成，帶動美債利率大幅反彈；台灣央行將於下週召開理監事會

利率面臨走升壓力 ， 預估5 年券區間為 1.83%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.93%~2%。

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on China's manufacturing PMI, which we
see as a potential catalyst for AirTAC's and Hiwin's share prices.
China's manufacturing PMI edged up  to 50.1 in September from 49.8 in August:
• This came in consistent with both our estimate and consensus of 50.1.
• The production index was up 1.3ppt while the new order index was down

0.1ppt MoM.
• Our macro team believes the upturn was led mainly led by a quarter-end
production push (see their report).
Our view:
• We believe the modest pickup in the  manufacturing PMI should help
sentiment  toward relevant stocks with high exposure to the Chinese market.
Among the automation names, we prefer plays with company-specific drivers
like AirTAC and Hiwin:
° AirTAC (1590.TW, OW) should continue to benefit from its own share
gains and incremental contribution from linear guides. Valuation appears

undemanding at 17x 2027e P/E (vs. the 25x average since 2020).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show more margin expansion in coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside at the current 29x 2027e P/E vs. peak cycle
valuation of 35-40x.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 30, 2026 05:15 PM GMT

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

toward CY27/28 with pricing upside
Migration in raw materials of CCL
Spec advancements in resin more debatable
Major raw materials for building CCL (copper clad laminate) include cloth, resin, and
copper foil. Glass cloth serves as grounding and offers support to the CCL and PCB
(printed circuit board) structure, and it is immersed in resins built on different in-house
chemical recipes by CCL makers to obtain PP (prepreg). Afterwards, CCL makers

add/laminate the copper foil on this intermediate product (PP), to form the CCL supplied
to PCB customers. Across the previous generations (Exhibit 2), we have seen rising
penetration of PPE/PPO (polyphenylene ether/oxide) gradually replacing FR-4/ epoxy
resins throughout M4-M8 grade CCL, while hydrocarbon resin gradually gains traction
from M8-grade CCL. Yet, there is a debate on PTFE-based (polytetrafluoroethylene) CCL
vs hydrocarbon resin as the CCL grade advances into M9/M10.

As illustrated in Exhibit 4, we see a more diversified path of resin upgrades from M8-
grade CCL, while the advancement in copper foil and glass cloth seems to be more
certain. In general, we note that the mix of PPO vs hydrocarbon used is roughly 2:1 in
M8-grade CCL, and such proportion reverses to 1:2 in M9-grade CCL. Yet, the broader
range of resin options could potentially alter the competitive dynamics within the CCL
industry, since different vendors are promoting (or have edge) in different types of CCL

solutions. Or in other words, the likely penetration by PTFE-based solutions (pushed
mainly by NVIDIA) will favor the suppliers with experiences in handling relevant products
(though smaller market size such as base station), while, at the same time, it might
curtail the headroom for major Taiwanese suppliers, including EMC and TUC (Taiwan
Union), to grow.
PPO/BMI vs hydrocarbon in M8/M9; hydrocarbon vs PTFE in M9/M10
In our view, hydrocarbon resin exhibits an ultra-low dielectric constant (Dk) of 2.2-2.6

and a dissipation factor (Df) reaching 0.001-0.005, as low as 0.0005 at high frequencies
(e.g., 10 GHz and above), thanks to its non-polar molecular backbone. However, there are
drawbacks to hydrocarbon resin, including rather limited flexibility and high brittleness
after curing, which necessitate the mix with ductile polymers such as PPO or BMI
(bismaleimide) to improve the toughness (peel strength). Nonetheless, the performance
in Dk/Df would be reduced after adding PPO/BMI to hydrocarbon resin, which makes the

solution not that favorable in meeting customers’ demand/requirement.
Within hydrocarbon resin, we note there are two major advanced versions, including ODV
(oxidative vulcanization; lower-end) and BCB (benzocyclobutene; higher-end), and key
suppliers for the latter include Dow Chemical and Asahi Kasei. In terms of Df, <0.0008
can be reached by BCB type of hydrocarbon resin based on the standard of M9-grade
CCL, and the number would hit <0.0005 based on the standard of M9+/M10-grade. By

CCL suppliers, we note EMC (Elite Material) adopts hydrocarbon/BMI resin in M9-grade
CCL, while Panasonic blends PPO or BMI (with hydrocarbon) in M9 (vs PPO/hydrocarbon
in M7/M8). Shengyi Technology also adopts PPO resin in M9-grade offering.
Given the disadvantages (discussed above) of PPO resin, some CCL providers
alternatively also adopt mPPO (modified PPO; Exhibit 5) by blending the PPO with other
resins (e.g., epoxy methacrylate, styrene). By doing so, the overall dielectric and thermal

properties of the composite are enhanced, and the reduced molecular weight improves
solubility in solvents like toluene and MEK (methyl ethyl ketone). This in turn enables the
resin to thermoset, and it becomes more suitable for CCL processing. By application, we
believe it is adopted in the LPU (language processing unit) and UBB (universal
baseboard) under Vera Rubin platform, 1.6T networking switch, and will also be
potentially (competing with PTFE-based solutions) adopted in the backplane.

### Page 3

Similar to what we have seen in PPO/PPE vs hydrocarbon in M8/M9, we believe
hydrocarbon and PTFE will coexist and complement each other in M9/M10-grade CCL,
while the sheer adoption on one side is unlikely, given 1) different consideration on
cost/performance by different customers; and 2) the difficulty in sourcing across resin
and the adjacent equipment in producing different types of CCL (e.g. vacuum hot press
machine). Meanwhile, we note that PPO/PPE cannot be paired with PTFE given the

conflicts in characteristics.
In addition to the specialized plasma treatment, and long lead time in pressing machine
(operating under high temperature) with high costs, one of the other major drawbacks to
PTFE is the lack of stiffness in texture which makes it hard to be processed under PCB
drilling/lamination. Thus, SiO2 (silicon dioxide) is used as a filler (or with increasing
consumption vs that in M7/M8-grade CCL) to fill the resin matrix, and this helps reduce

the thermal expansion coefficient (CTE) and enhance the mechanical strength of the
composites. Elsewhere, a lack of adhesion to copper foil is the other key technological
challenge when processing PTFE-based solutions, and this requires the help from
hydrocarbon resin (to enhance the adhesion).
In short, the pursuit of more advanced materials like PTFE/hydrocarbon will
simultaneously incur other technological challenges, which, in our view, makes relevant

solutions to become more versatile (to solve the associated challenges). In Exhibit 6, we
present the promoted solutions of M9/M10-grade CCL in the market, and we compare
different metrics, including signal loss, mechanical strength, processibility, and costs.
Given the low penetration by M9-grade CCL currently (evidenced by single-digit% of
industry leader EMC’s revenue), we believe none of these solutions has become
mainstream. In other words, we deem the ongoing development and qualification/testing

results worth monitoring, and these include the feedback from board, ODM (original
design manufacturer) and end-customers’ levels. In our view, the most imminent (and
perhaps critical) one will likely be the solution adopted by Rubin Ultra switch board and
backplane (if any), which could be finally confirmed by end-2026/early-2027.

Exhibit 5: A comparison between PPO and mPPO 
mPPO carries enhanced processability and reactivity when compared with PPO 
Characteristics/type of 
resin PPO mPPO 
Dk Inherently low. Values for PPO-based composites are typically in the range of 2.1 - 3.5.  Retains low Dk, with high-performance grades achieving tightly controlled values, e.g., 2.8 - 3.0 for M9 grade. 
Df Inherently very low. Values for PPO-based systems can be as low as 0.0006. Significantly reduced and optimized. Ranges from 0.004 – 0.008 for M6/M7 grades down to <0.002 (typically 0.0015–0.002) for ultra-low loss M9 grades. 
Processability Limited. High melt viscosity and lack of reactivity make it difficult to process.  Better. Low molecular weight and reactive end-groups enable good solubility, compatibility with CCL manufacturing, and thermosetting cross-linking. 
Reactivity Low. Lacks sufficient reactive groups to cross-link effectively with other resins. High. Functionalized end-groups (e.g., styrene, methacrylate) allow it to chemically bond with other components in the resin formulation. 
Source: BofA Global Research

Clearer upgrades in glass cloth and copper foil  
While the upgrades in resin system appear to be more debatable with a versatility in 
solutions, we witness a firmer roadmap advancement in glass cloth and copper foil. For 
glass cloth, (see reports) Nittobo shared the view of 100% demand growth in NER this 
year (FY3/27), followed by another c60% growth next year. As for copper foil, Mitsui 
Kinzoku also revised up the mix of HVLP4 and above products in 1H26 (within very 
smooth profile copper foil) to 45% (was 40%), and the figure is expected to reach 60% 
in 2H26. On pricing, we note that Mitsui Kinzoku also mentioned it would try pursuing a 
higher ASP in product segments where demand remains strong, while there is a higher 
processing fee (Exhibit 7) charged by copper foil suppliers in the high-end copper foil 
market. All-in-all, we see positive indications to the high-end CCL pricing, given that the 
CCL makers will try to pass through the inflated costs (due to stronger demand and the 
premium in processing fee) to the product ASP.

Ongoing spec migration on multiple fronts into CY27-28 
We see a clear upgrade cycle for CCL materials and PCB production technique across 
networking switches and AI (artificial intelligence) accelerators, including GPUs (graphic 
processing unit) and ASICs (application specific integrated circuits). In our view, the spec 
advancement (Exhibit 8) is primarily driven by faster data rates and increasingly 
stringent signal-integrity requirements. For NVIDIA, GB200/GB300 largely adopt M8-
grade CCL, with low-Dk1/2 glass cloth and HVLP2/3 copper foil. The transition to VR200 
drives further CCL specification upgrades toward M8+/M9-grade, along with the 
adoption of HVLP4 copper foil in VR200 compute board and midplane, while LPU adopts 
Q cloth with less signal loss (vs low Dk2).  
While NVIDIA tends to place greater emphasis on glass cloth upgrades, ASICs and 
networking switches are more focused on copper foil migration. Among ASICs, we 
expect the CCL spec for Google TPU (tensor processing unit) v8 to migrate to M8+

based on low Dk2 cloth and HVLP3 copper foil (vs M7-grade CCL with low Dk1 and
HVLP2 for TPU v7), followed by M9-grade adoption in TPU v9 based on HVLP4 copper
foil (with either low Dk2 or Q-cloth). As for the Trainium chips by Amazon, there is a
more visible upgrade in copper foil (vs glass cloth) taking place in Trainium 3 (2026),
while its OAM (open accelerator module) in Trainium 4 (2027) will likely be upgraded to
M8-grade CCL based on low Dk2 cloth. Simultaneously, we also expect a migration

toward HDI (high-density interconnect) from HLC (high-layer count), with regard to the
PCB production for the UBB (universal baseboard) in TPU v9 and Trainium 4.

Along with the data-rate migration toward 800G and beyond (from 100/200G) for optical
module, PCB structure evolves from conventional tenting design toward more
complicated mSAP/SAP (semi-additive process) with higher layer count, enabling finer
L/S (line/space) and smaller pitch (Exhibit 9). To elaborate, L/S of 3.2T optical module
should shrink materially to 15/15μm from 60/60μm at 800G, and it was 125/125um for

100G generation. Simultaneously, the layer count would also increase to up to layers for
3.2T products, vs 10-layer in 400G generation.
Amid the backdrop of rapid spec migration, major PCB suppliers are accelerating
capacity expansion to capture the burgeoning demand in high-end optical module
(Exhibit 10), and these include Avary under ZDT (Zhen Ding Technology), Founder, SCC
(Shennan Circuits), Unimicron and Compeq. While Unimicron remains the biggest

supplier of optical module PCB (printed circuit board) in 2H26, we flag Avary and SCC
could potentially become the other major suppliers in 2027, given their more aggressive
capacity increase.

Expect further ASP uplift driven by mix & cost inflation 
We expect CCL (for PCB) names’ revenue growth to be fueled by continuous capacity 
expansion and ASP uplifts, and these include EMC, TUC, Shengyi Tech and Kingboard 
Laminates among our coverage. The ASP increase, in our view, will be driven by both 
price hike on a like-for-like basis (given the cost push) and mix improvements to higher 
CCL grades, and we note the former/latter represent bigger boost to the near-
term/long-term ASP. To elaborate, the difference in near-term vs long-term boost can 
be explained by 1) a shorter production cycle time of less than 1-2 weeks by CCL makers 
which normally carry less than a quarter of inventory; and 2) a continuous spec 
advancement in CCL material grade given the requirement in data-transmission.  
In Exhibit 11-13, we decipher the revenue growth by different CCL names based on the 
planned capacity expansion and ASP uplift, as well as the assumption of 95%/90%/90% 
capacity utilization rate by EMC/TUC/Shengyi Tech in 2028. In our view, the implied ASP 
growth of 4%/11%/18% by respective names look conservative, and we see room for 
further upside in the case of continuous cost inflation from the raw materials (e.g. glass 
cloth, copper foil). Further, we believe a smaller magnitude of ASP increase by EMC (vs 
TUC/Shengyi Tech) in 2028 can be explained by its already significant exposure to high-
end CCL market in 2027. For reference, we present the CCL ASP by each grade with the 
figure for M4-grade indexed to 100 in Exhibit 20, and the gap of ASP by each grade 
remains quite meaningful, in our view.  
Regarding the CCL mix by grade, we break down the revenue contribution by different 
grades to each company throughout 2025-28, and we compare the numbers to their 
gross margins in Exhibit 14-19. When comparing the gross margin, we carve out the 
revenue mix from M7-grade and above CCL, given that M6-grade is already in supply-
demand balance (or slightly oversupply) situation from late-2025.

### Page 8

Among the three names, we expect EMC to remain the industry leader with 
73%/85%/92% revenue mix from M7-grade and above CCL in 2026/27/28, which helps 
strengthen its gross margin to 35.8%/36.7% in 2027/28 (vs 34% in 2026). TUC will 
follow suit with 43%/47%/53% revenue mix from M7-grade and above CCL in 
2026/27/28, with gross margin edging toward 32.6%/33.7% in 2027/28 (vs 30.4% in 
2026). As for Shengyi Tech, we expect 28%/35%/57% revenue mix from M7-grade and 
above CCL in 2026/27/28, and its gross margin (in CCL business) is expected to inch up 
to 31%/31.8 in 2027/28 (vs 30.7% in 2026).

 BofA GLOBAL RESEARCH 
Regarding the ASP uplifts by firm in 2027, we further decode into the factors of cost 
inflation and mix enhancement as shown in Exhibit 21. To simply put, we expect a 
smaller boost to EMC (vs TUC/Shengyi Tech) from cost inflation of raw materials and 
the corresponding pass-through, given its relatively lower revenue exposure to low-/mid-
end CCL market, where CCL makers pass through the cost more frequently upon 
squeezed margin profile. Yet, on the other hand, EMC (in terms of ASP) is expected to 
benefit more from the enhanced mix in 2027, thanks to the further ramp of M8/M8.5-
grade CCL as well as the penetration increase of M9 and PTFE-based solutions.  
Year-to-date, we believe there is already 40%+/20%+ price hike across low-/high-end 
CCL, and we do not rule out the likelihood of further price increase given the (see report) 
expectations of continuously rising copper price into late-2026 and 2027 (Exhibit 22). 
There was a price hike of VSP (very smooth profile) foil, effective from July 2026 by 
Mitsui Kinzoku given the demand strength and likely to be followed suit by a Taiwanese 
supplier (Co-Tech), based on our checks with supply chain. On the glass cloth side, we 
note the potential pricing upside from low Dk2 cloth given the supply shortage, while the 
price of low-end E-glass cloth (such as 7628 type) remains on the uptrend (Exhibit 23) 
and may reach CNY15-16 (per msq) by end-2026. In our view, all of these point to a 
benign pricing outlook for CCL makers.  
To pinpoint the benefits from price increase by CCL makers, we conduct a scenario 
analysis to each name’s 2027 bottom-line as shown in Exhibit 24. In short, we expect 
1.2-1.6% upside to the net income estimates coming from every 1ppt price hike, if such 
price increase purely reflects cost inflation. On the other hand, we expect 3.3-5.1% 
upside to the net income estimates, if the price hike is purely driven a supply shortage 
(which enables CCL makers to raise the ASP proactively). Further on Shengyi Tech, we 
assume that its PCB business (Shengyi Electronics) can also pass on the cost increase 
(from CCL side), with same magnitude to its ASP.  
For Kingboard Laminates, its vertical integration significantly enhances its earnings 
sensitivity to price hikes, as most of its key raw materials are produced in-house. 
Notably, price hikes in glass fiber cloth can translate almost entirely into profit, given in-
house production of yarn. Our analysis suggests that 100% increase in CCL prices could 
drive c.28ppt gross margin expansion for KBL, and more than 200% growth at the EPS 
level in 2026. This assumes that future price hikes are primarily driven by cloth 
tightness, while input costs for copper foil and resin remain relatively stable.

Sector view: stay positive on industry leaders  
We stay constructive on the CCL for PCB area, given the ongoing spec upgrades and 
robust demand growth into 2027-28, and we believe the worries from CPO (co-packaged 
optics) proliferation are either overstated or distant, as the mass adoption of CPO to 
replace NPO (near-packaged optics) or pluggable optical modules will not take place until 
2029/30, at earliest.  
Currently, we see extended lead-time (of production) toward 2-3 months by high-end 
CCL makers in 2H26 (vs 1-2 months seen in 1H26), while low/mid-end suppliers’ lead 
time is also extended to 1-2 months (7-10 days before). We also believe the current 
valuation by EMC (21-22x 2027E P/E) and TUC (17-18x 2027E P/E) looks compelling, 
given that the numbers do not seem lofty when compared with the historical trading 
range of 1) 15-20x before mid-2023; and 2) normally above 20x after mid-2023 when 
the strong demand from AI server starts to emerge. As for Kingboard Laminates, it is 
traded at 8x 2027E P/E, below historical average of 10x. 
Among our coverage, we highlight Buy-rated EMC, given its technology leadership in 
high-end CCL area along with diversified customer profile across GPU and ASIC 
accounts, as well as TUC, given its (see report) aggressive capacity expansion into 2027 
and beyond. We also highlight Buy rating on Doosan Corp and Kingboard Laminates, 
given the former’s solid position in NVIDIA GPU and Amazon switch, as well as the 
latter’s robust earnings expansion amid CCL price hike and rising high-end contribution 
starting 2027. As for Shengyi Tech, we remain relatively conservative, given its relatively 
slower expansion in CCL capacity along with a milder growth in the PCB business.

EMC (2383 TT; Buy; PO NT$8,200) 
We reckon EMC’s potential share loss in Rubin Ultra switch board (tray), given the 
competition in PTFE-based (hybrid) solutions (vs M9-Q), as well as the ongoing debate 
between PTFE and quartz-glass/hydrocarbon resin solutions into M10-grade CCL. We 
also note that M9-Q (where EMC has more notable technology leadership) is likely to be 
adopted in backplane by NVIDIA, no matter if it will initially be seen in Rubin Ultra or 
Feynman generation. Net-net, we believe the impact on bottom-line (if any) from 
aforementioned headwinds should be confined to LSD (low-single-digit)%, considering 
1) 80-90k (per month) of relevant demand by NVIDIA’s switch board vs EMC’s capacity 
of ~6/9mn in 2026/27; and 2) the demand backfill from other high-end projects across 
AMD and other ASIC customers.  
Looking ahead, we see further expansion plan beyond 1H28 as a catalyst, if it is 
announced. In addition to the CCL for PCB business, we note that substrate CCL 
business boasts at least 50-60% GM (gross margin), and EMC could expand relevant 
capacity to 1.2mn sheet /month which could help expedite its market-share gain toward 
~5% (vs 1-2% in 2025). We raise 2026/27/28E EPS by 5%/11%/14% after penciling in 
stronger revenue growth and gross margin profile, while our PO stays at NT$8,200 as 
we apply a lower P/E multiple of 30x (was 34x), with the same valuation period of 2H27-
1H28E, mainly reflecting the overhang from competition in PTFE-based solutions.

TUC (6274 TT; Buy; PO NT$2,600) 
Looking into 2027, we flag multiple positives from spec advancements for TUC, 
including 1) the potentially partial adoption of M9-grade CCL by 1.6T networking switch, 
where TUC’s solution is based on low Dk3 glass cloth; 2) the upgrade to M8-grade CCL 
for LEO (low earth orbit); and 3) the migration toward M8-grade material by Vera CPU 
(central processing unit). Meanwhile, we share the view that hybrid solution from PTFE-
based CCL may last toward M10-grade. Demand from high-end optical module (800G 
and above) could have remained underestimated at current stage, as we believe relevant 
TAM (total addressable market) for CCL could reach NT$30bn+, which represents close 
to 50% of TUC’s total revenue in 2026. In terms of Thailand operations, we believe it 
could turn breakeven or profitable from 3Q26, thanks to the smooth ramp of 
capacity/output to pair with the robust demand growth outlook from customers.  
We raise 2026/27/28E EPS by 7%/11%/7% after baking in the assumptions of stronger 
revenue growth and slightly enhanced GM profile as well as operating leverage. Our PO 
stays at NT$2,600, based on a lower P/E multiple of 27x (was 30.5x) and an unchanged 
valuation period of 2H27-1H28E. We attribute the cut in valuation multiple to the likely 
growing traction by PTFE-based solutions that serve as other alternatives in the high-
end CCL market.

Shengyi Technology (600183 CH; U/P; PO CNY99) 
We believe revenue contribution from PTFE-based CCL could reach ~10% based on the 
assumption of US$300-400 ASP per piece, and we expect the ramp of such business to 
bolster the overall margin profile thanks to ~50% GM by relevant solutions. Yet we also 
note that the firm’s capacity for M6-grade and above CCL remains relatively limited at 
~10% mix currently, and further capacity ramp will be dependent on the progress in 
Thailand and Songshan Lake (for M7-M9), after its Jiangxi phase 2 capacity became fully 
loaded after 1H26.  
We raise 2026/27/28E EPS by 29%/21%/18% after baking in 2Q26 results and the 
assumptions of stronger revenue growth and margin profiles, which also reflect its 
further share gain in NVIDIA’s AI server system/platforms. Our PO goes to CNY99 (from 
CNY83), same based on 24x 2027E P/E, and the volatility risk rating is changed to C 
from B.

Valuation discussion  
Both Buy-rated EMC and TUC are expected to post stronger EPS growth toward 2028 
along with a higher dividend yield. However, they trade at lower P/E multiples when 
compared with peers’ average in CCL industry. As for P/B multiples, the premium 
valuation by these two names, in our view, can be explained by much higher ROE 
expected in 2027-28. On the other hand, our more conservative view on Shengyi can be 
supported by its more stretched valuation (for both P/E and P/B) vs milder EPS growth 
and lower dividend yield, when compared with the peers’ average. Overall, we believe the 
(see report) overhang from CPO proliferation is already largely reflected in the valuation 
of CCL names, which we believe also renders an attractive opportunity for EMC/TUC, 
considering their solid earnings growth ahead fueled by continuous mix upgrades and 
more aggressive capacity expansion.

Price objective basis & risk 
Doosan Corp (DOSAF) 
Our PO of W1,800,000 is derived from SOTP valuation, which implies a fair market cap 
of W32tn = W20tn for core businesses (based on 20x 2027-28E P/E, which is in line with

### Page 22

the average of CCL peers) + W12tn in listed equity investments (Doosan Enerbility, 
Doosan Robotics, and Oricom) + net debt (breakeven) + other investments (W0.25tn, 
mostly non-listed affiliates). Our W1,800,000 PO implies 8x 2027-28E P/B, which 
appears much higher than the 2023-25 average of 4x, as well as a relatively high P/E 
(31x based on 2027-28E EPS) versus other Korea Holdco companies (5-10x). Key reason 
behind our relatively higher fair multiples compared to its historical average and its 
peers should be Doosan's rapidly growing CCL business thanks to its dominant market 
share position within NVIDIA.

Upside risks: (1) further market share gains in high-end CCL for major US hyperscalers, 
(2) a more favorable CCL pricing environment driven by sustained demand growth and 
tight industry supply, (3) incremental demand opportunities from emerging technologies 
such as co-packaged optics (CPO) and other networking equipment, and (4) faster-than-
expected capacity ramp-up.

Downside risks: (1) a slowdown in AI infrastructure spending or cuts to US hyperscaler 
capex, which could weaken demand for high-end CCL used in AI servers and networking 
equipment, (2) intensifying competition and pricing pressure in the advanced CCL 
market, and (3) potential delays in Doosan's CCL capacity expansion. 
Elite Material (ETMCF) 
Our PO of NT$8,200 is based on 30x 2H27-1H28E P/E, at the mid-to-high-end of its 
historical range of 6-34x since 2016. In our view, the multiple is appropriate, given the 
market-share expansion in server/networking, where we see structural growth in 
addressable market.

Upside risks are (1) share gain at substrate CCL, (2) stronger 5G smartphone shipment, 
(3) greater 5G smartphone spec upgrades, and (4) favorable FX and raw material cost 
change.

Downside risks are (1) share loss at iPhone, (2) keener competition in high-end CCL, and 
(3) unfavorable FX and raw material cost change. 
Kingboard Laminates (KGBLF) 
We set our PO at HK$65, on 13x 2H26-1H27E P/E. 13x is around +0.5SD of KBL's 
historical trading range, and matches with its last earnings upcycle in 2021 (led by e-
glass price hike) while also reflects rising mix from high-end AI-related deliveries.

Downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected CCL price hike, (2) better-than-expected 
industry supply of upstream materials esp. cloth, (3) slower-than-expected AI server 
demand, and (4) slower-than-expected external glass fiber cloth sales.

Upside risks: (1) stronger-than-expected CCL price hike, (2) slower-than-expected 
industry supply of upstream materials esp. cloth, (3) stronger-than-expected AI server 
demand, and (4) faster-than-expected external glass fiber cloth sales. 
Shengyi (DGUNF) 
Our PO of CNY99 is based on 24x 2027E P/E, close to the mid-to-high-end range of the 
historical average of 10-35x in 2017-2024. In our view, such valuation multiple is backed 
by operating margin and ROE of 23% and 37% in 2026-28E (vs 11% and 12% in 2023-
25).

Upside risks to our price objective are: (1) better macro conditions, (2) faster 5G 
deployment, (3) less-intensive competition, and (4) favorable FX and raw material cost 
changes.

### Page 23

Downside risks are: (1) worse macro conditions, (2) slower 5G deployment, (3) more 
severe competition, and (4) unfavorable FX and raw material cost changes. 
Taiwan Union Technology Corporation (TWUNF) 
Our PO NT$2,600 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is at the mid-to-high-end of 
the historical trading range of 8-31x during 2016-2026. In our view, the valuation 
multiple is justified, given operating margin and ROE expansion toward 26% and 63% in 
2026-28E (vs 13% and 16% in 2023-25) along with robust free-cash flow generation in 
over the same period.

Downside risks are 
1) Fluctuation in raw material prices, which could lead to a more volatile gross margin 
for TUC in the near term 
2) More intensified competition in CCL, which could pressure TUC's overall margin 
profile 
3) Adoption of new type of upstream material (e.g., quartz), which could bring business 
uncertainty for the names in CCL industry, including TUC 
4) Worse-than-expected execution by the company, which could cause market-share loss 
and/or decreased business traction with customers

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment rating 
of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, the 0-
12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a stock 
should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst ’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

Editorial 
A Fed credibility-induced bounce  
Strategy summary:  The Fed’s unanimous decision to hike 
rates this week for the first time since 2023 , and pencil in 
another hike by December, confirms our expectations – flagged 
in the last two weeklies - of a central bank willing to establish its 
independence against political pressure. The Fed’s moderately 
hawkish pivot is helping restore its inflation-fighting credentials. 
The BoJ also hiked rates this week. It is the first time on record 
all three major central banks – Fed, ECB and BoJ – have hiked 
in the same month , as they respond primarily to a resurgence 
in energy-driven inflation amid tight job markets.  
We expect the Fed to hike once more by year-end to 4.0-4.25% 
and follow up with another hike in H1 2027 to 4.25 -4.5%. With 
the revised estimates, we revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts. Nevertheless, with Fed policy uncertainty 
easing, we see an opportunity to use the equity market dip to 
add exposure amid solid corporate earnings.  
Fed moves to restore credibility:  The Fed’s policy pivot this 
week followed Chair Warsh’s hawkish Jackson Hole speech 
and recent economic data prints, namely: a pick -up in job 
creation and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in 
oil prices due to the re -escalation of the Middle E ast conflict. 
Given the unanimous Fed decision to hike rates this week, we 
expect two more hikes by  June 2027 on the back of  robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated. We expect this pivot to help the Fed 
rebuild credibility as an inflation fighter.  
Policy outlook less aggressive than market pricing : The 
updated Fed policy rate estimates are lower compared with 
money market pricing of three more rate hikes by June 2027 
since we expect disinflation to take hold by Q2 2027 as the 
impact of oil prices and tariffs fade. We believe policy rates can 
return to the 4.0–4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility 
of cuts in H2 2027 as inflation pressures ease.  
Upgrading near-term US rates and USD estimates : Based 
on these revised estimates, we also upgrade our 3-month target 
for the US 10 -year government bond yield to 5.0 -5.25%. We 
also revise up our 12 -month target for the US 10 -year bond 
yield to 4.75-5.0% (see page 4).  
Buying the equity dip as focus turns to fundamentals: Fed 
hikes typically do not hurt equities over the medium-term when 
accompanied by strong earnings growth. We estimate a 25bps 
rise in Fed rates (the discount rate) would impact the S&P500 
index and global equities by 3 -4%. However, we expect the 
headwind from higher rates to be offset by strong earnings 
growth, driving positive performance for global equities.  
Furthermore, a hawkish Fed is largely priced in and we expect 
the Fed to deliver fewer rate hikes than currently priced by 
markets. Meanwhile, we believe concerns over AI industry 
leaders slowing the pace of development are overdone. Hence, 
we maintain our preference for a diversified group of large cap 
technology and semiconductor industry leaders (see page 5-6). 
BoJ hikes; modestly bullish JPY : The BoJ hiked rates by 
25bps to a three-decade high of 1.25%, as expected, but the 7-
2 split vote led to renewed JPY weakness on concerns it may 
slow the pace of further hikes. We remain constructive on JPY 
amid narrowing rate differential vs peers as the BoJ potentially 
hikes rates by 25bps once per quarter until Q2 2027 to curb 
rising domestic wage-driven inflation pressure . Any sign of BoJ 
slowing the pace of hikes could lead to renewed JPY weakness, 
fuelling inflationary pressures (see page 7).  
BoE holds; rangebound GBP: The BoE held rates this week, 
bucking the hiking trend among peers, as weakening UK job 
and housing markets offset rebound in energy-driven inflation. 
We expect BoE to deliver fewer rate hikes than the 100bps of 
increase currently priced by markets  as the job market slows 
further. This is likely to keep GBP rangebound (see page 7).  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

Top client questions 
  Where do you see US government bond  yields following 
the Fed’s 16 September decision to hike interest rates? 
Our view: The US 10-year government bond yield is likely to 
retest the 5.00-5.25% range in the near term. We favour 3-7-year 
maturity bond s in t he US, preferring credit over government 
bonds. We are looking for an opportunity to extend duration 
approaching Q2 2027. 
Rationale: Over the next few months, we expect 2- and 30-year US 
government bond yields to remain rangebound, but the 10-year yield 
is expected to rebound up to the 5.00-5.25% range. The reasons for 
this include: 1) oil prices staying elevated and potentially rise further 
due to the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, with Saudi 
Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline shut, the Houthis now firmly in control 
of the Strait of Bab-al-Mandab, the Strait of Hormuz still closed and 
China returning as a buyer of crude oil in the market ; 2) despite 
notable AI leaders expressing the need to slow AI development, AI 
capex plans are showing no signs of slowing down, which continues 
to drive above-trend growth in the economy. This, in turn, also means 
AI-related heavy bond issuance  is expected to continue. These 
issuers typically issue long-dated tenors, and this competition for 
capital will also exert upward pressure on the US 10-year yields. 
We note that the US 10-year bond yield has room to move lower, 
driven by short-covering activities in the very near term as net-short 
positions had moved to extreme levels before th is week’s  Fed 
meeting. However, we think such downshifts would be short-lived 
and the central tendency for the 10-year yield would be to move back 
up to the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Meanwhile, we see US 30-year yields continuing to reflect the dire 
fiscal outlook for the US government. We expect US 2-year yields 
to continue trading in a range that reflects expectations of further 
federal funds rate hikes. For the record, we only expect two more 
rate hikes from the Fed into the middle of 2027 , while money 
markets are pricing in three more for the same period. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect oil prices to moderate (either 
through a resolution of the M iddle East conflict or a re-routing of oil 
supply chains)  and the inflationary effects from tariffs to fade. 
Inflation by then is likely be close to – if not at – the Fed’s target of 
2%. At this point, we see room for the Fed to contemplate rate 
cuts. US 2-year yields (the tenor that is most sensitive to Fed policy 
changes) will likely start to price th is in. With oil prices and inflation 
cooling, 10-year yields would also have room to come down. 
US 30-year yields, on the other hand, are expected to  remain 
elevated as the US fiscal deficit remains substantial next year and 
could become much worse than its already dire state today. As a 
result, the ‘term premium ’ may keep the ultra -long end elevated. 
Short of some unorthodox policy to artificially cap them lower, 30 -
year yields could continue to drift higher into H2 2027. 
We continue to favour the 3-7-year segment of the yield curve and 
prefer credit over government bond s. We see an opportunity for 
us to extend duration closer to Q2 2027. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO for Fixed Income and FX

We have updated our three- and 12-month outlook 
for US government bond yields

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Will the step -up in the  Fed’s risk-free rate trigger a re -
pricing of equities? 
Our view: We expect strong corporate earnings growth to offset 
the headwind of higher rates , driving positive performance for 
global equities. We remain Overweight global equities. 
Rationale: The Fed’s latest 25bps rate hike had been anticipated 
by the bond market, partly reflected in the bond yield  rise we have 
seen so far in 2026. Using the US 10-year government bond yield as 
a proxy for the risk -free rate, the yield has risen by 80-85bps this 
year. We estimate that each 25bps rise in the discount rate presents 
a valuation drag on global equities. On this basis, the rise in yields 
represents a potential 10-14% headwind for equities this year. 
However, the Fed’s hike reflects a solid expansion of US economic 
activity, resilient domestic spending and robust capital investment. 
This is consistent with the positive earnings revisions we have seen 
this year, with  2026 earnings growth projections now at 34% (and 
16% for 2027). Global equities are up 14% year-to-date (YTD), but 
even with a 14% valuation headwind from higher yields, we believe 
they can rise further, given strong earnings growth in 2026 and 2027. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is the outlook for the US 10-year government bond 
yield? What is your tactical view on the DXY? 
Our view: We raise our three-month US 10 -year government 
bond yield forecast to the 5.00-5.25% range. Meanwhile, we see 
a near-term upward bias in the DXY after its recent downtrend, 
supported by recent Fed tightening and higher  short-term US 
bond yields.  
Rationale: The Fed 's decision  to raise the target range for the 
federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75-4.00% validates the upside policy 
risks we flagged recently, following Fed Chair Warsh's Jackson Hole 
speech and a run of firmer US economic data. We expect growth 
to remain robust, the labour market  to remain tight and inflation to 
stay elevated, supporting one more hike before year-end 2026 and 
potentially another in H1 2027  – fewer than the three additional 
hikes currently priced in by money markets . For the US 10-year 
government bond yield, further policy tightening and lingering US 
fiscal concerns should keep yields elevated, but the Fed’s visible 
response to inflation should limit additional upward pressure, leaving 
the 10-year yield holding around the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Crucially, Warsh provided no forward guidance on further rate 
hikes. Markets had already priced in substantial additional rate hikes 
ahead of the Fed meeting, reducing the scope for further USD upside 
purely from rate repricing. Meanwhile, we expect further policy 
tightening from the ECB and the BoJ, while the RBA also remains 
biased towards further tightening. This should gradually narrow the 
US rate advantage and limit the extent of a DXY rebound. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

Global equities’ earnings growth estimates have 
been revised higher this year, offsetting the 
headwind from higher rates

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in technology as AI lab 
CEOs call for a slowdown in frontier AI model development? 
Our view: We believe concerns over “pacing the frontier” are 
overdone. We maintain our preference to remain invested in 
and diversified across Big Tech and semiconductor leaders. 
Rationale: We believe it is prudent for investors to avoid 
overreacting to the recent calls for a slowdown in frontier AI model 
development, as they focus on responsible AI development, rather 
than halting AI model development or reducing spending. The global 
AI leadership  race, reinforced by the US and China’s  strategic 
ambitions, also makes a coordinated slowdown unlikely. 
While the near-term narrative may shift from AI training beneficiaries 
towards inference plays, hyperscalers continue to signal strong AI 
infrastructure commitments and improving monetisation, supporting 
our AI capex outlook. Rising AI adoption and agentic use cases 
should sustain inference demand, even if training activity moderates, 
benefiting custom AI chips, NAND flash memory and hyperscalers.  
We believe the decline in valuations  this year more than 
compensates for any downside risk to AI capex. Our Opportunistic 
idea on Global Semiconductors further reflects our conviction, with 
attractive valuations supporting selective additions during volatility. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  With ASEAN and UK equities outperforming AI-focused 
markets in H2 2026, is it worth adding to these markets? 
Our view: ASEAN and UK equities offer portfolio diversification, 
but we are Underweight both markets in favour of US and Asia 
ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, given their stronger earnings potential. 
Rationale: The ASEAN and UK markets have outperformed the 
MSCI ACWI over the past three months, but both have 
underperformed on a YTD basis. Financials is the largest sector in 
the ASEAN (53%) and the UK (28%) and provides steady earnings. 
However, both markets have minimal exposure to the technology 
sector (just 3% and 1% , respectively), ultimately dragging down 
growth. Hence, while defensive markets offer value by increasing 
diversification and reducing volatility in a portfolio, we maintain our 
Underweight allocation for ASEAN and the UK, as we see much 
stronger earnings potential elsewhere in the US and AxJ markets.  
We remain Overweight US and AxJ, supported by robust earnings 
growth, as  AI still has the potential to become a major economic 
growth driver . Our 12-month S&P500 Index forecast of 8,400  
implies low-teens upside, supported by a strong earnings backdrop 
of 25-30% earnings-per-share growth for 2026 and 15 -20% for 
2027. AxJ also boasts the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, fuelled by the AI semiconductor super-cycle. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is it still appropriate to have a positive stance on US 
high-yield (HY) bonds? 
Our view: We remain opportunistically bullish on US HY bonds. 
Lower sensitivity to interest rate volatility, a below-trend default 
rate and a maturity wall pushed out to 2028-29 drive this view. 
Rationale: Recent interest rate volatility has highlighted a key 
structural advantage of US HY bonds: their higher coupon rates  
and shorter tenors relative to Developed Market (DM) government 
bonds and US investment-grade (IG) corporate bonds translate into 
a much lower sensitivity to interest rates, cushioning bond prices 
during yield backup episodes, where bond yields rise sharply. 
YTD, US HY has delivered 1.8% in total returns, outperforming DM 
government bonds and US IG corporate bonds by over 3% each on 
a relative basis. This outperformance was particularly evident during 
the recent back-up in bond yields, as inflation concerns re-emerged 
after renewed Middle East tensions – an environment where HY's 
lower rate sensitivity provided a relative cushion  even as broader 
fixed income markets came under pressure in absolute terms. On 
the fundamentals side, US HY corporate credit quality remains 
resilient. Default rates continue to track below long-run trend levels, 
and the maturity wall has been pushed out to 2028 -29, reducing 
near-term refinancing risk. Against a non -recessionary macro 
backdrop, we expect these credit fundamentals to hold, supporting 
the case for continued opportunistic exposure to the asset class.  
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Do the latest BoJ and BoE meetings alter your view on the 
central banks’ rate paths and their corresponding currencies? 
Our view: We remain constructive on the JPY and expect two 
BoJ rate hikes this year and potentially one hike per quarter in 
H1 2027 as policy normalisation continues. Meanwhile, the BoE 
held rates at 3.75%. We expect GBP/USD to stay rangebound 
within 1.32-1.36 over the next 1-3 months, with downside risk 
towards the lower end of our medium-term 1.30-1.36 range. 
Rationale: Persistent inflation, higher energy prices and earlier JPY 
weakness support further BoJ policy normalisation . Continued 
hiking should progressively narrow the US -Japan rate gap, reduce 
the attractiveness of JPY -funded carry trades and support  the JPY 
over the medium term towards the 153-155 range. However, with a 
September hike largely priced  in, future tightening ’s pace  will be 
more important for the JPY. Meanwhile, the BoE was split 6-3 in its 
decision to hold rates at 3.75% , with three members preferring a 
25bps hike, signalling elevated inflation risks. However, a weaker 
labour market is likely to limit the pace of BoE tightening. The US-
UK rate divergence is expected to favour the USD over the next 1-3 
months. Further BoE tightening (if inflation remains elevated ) could 
narrow the rate gap and provide support to the GBP later in the cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

We expect US HY bonds to continue delivering 
strong performance in the near term

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The Cotality home value index, covering all of Australia,
fell 1.1% in September with downward revisions to the
previous two months again shaving a further 0.4% off prices.
The correction has now seen prices decline 5.2% from their

March peak. Annual growth is flat.
The price correction remains broad, Cotality reporting 97% of
capital city suburbs experienced falls over the three months
to September, Darwin the only city to record gains. Sydney
and Melbourne continue to see more pronounced declines,
albeit moderating a touch, particularly in Melbourne.
Against this, declines in Brisbane, Adelaide and Perth have
accelerated over the last few months. Across segments, unit
prices and lower price tiers continue to track slightly milder

falls than houses and top tiers.
Seasonal variations accentuated price weakness through winter
but are now neutral. The peak-to-trough price decline nationally
is slightly milder at 4.6% in seasonally adjusted terms. Unless
specified, figures in titles, tables and the commentary below are
in non-seasonally adjusted terms, consistent with the ‘headline’
figures reported by Cotality. However, figures shown in charts
are in seasonally adjusted terms.

Preliminary estimates of turnover show rolling 3mth volumes
down 3.9%qtr in September and 17.4%yr with previous
estimates revised 1-2ppts lower. The monthly pattern
suggests small gains in July and August have given way to
another sizeable fall in September.
Looking at the price performance across different jurisdictions:
• Sydney prices dropped 1.4% fall in September, monthly
declines averaging 1.6% over the previous four months,
tracking a 17% annualised pace of decline since May. All

segments and tiers recorded falls in Q3 but with top and
middle tier houses notably weaker.
• Melbourne prices fell 0.7%mth, a slower pace than other
capitals. This likely reflects the weaker starting point coming
into the correction, prices already back to the levels seen in
mid-2021. As elsewhere, 'top tier' houses prices are leading
the decline. 'Low tier' unit prices mostly held their ground in
Q3, prices in this sub-segment already plumbing 10yr lows.

• Brisbane was the weakest capital city price-wise, recording
a 1.5% drop in September with previous monthly declines
also revised down heavily (August from 1% to 1.6% and
July from 1.2% to 1.7%). The combined effect has taken
the rolling 3mth change to –4.7%qtr from –2.7%qtr last
month and –0.6%qtr in July. At 5.9%yr, annual growth is
still positive but losing altitude quickly, having run at 18%yr
as recently as May. Sellers have been slow to respond to

Losing altitude

3WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
the sudden stop with total listings up over 50% vs a year
ago, albeit still not high compared to long run averages
(reflecting the extremely tight starting point for the Brisbane
market).
• Adelaide dwelling prices fell 1.3%mth, the August decline

also marked down from 0.8% to 1.1%, annual price growth
dropping to 6.5%yr. The price correction began later in
Adelaide but is now running at a similar pace to prices
nationally.
• Perth recorded a 1.2% decline but also saw big downward
revisions to previous months (August from 0.8% to –1.7%
and July from –1.3% to –1.8%). At 10.1%yr, annual growth
is still solidly positive but was tracking at over 25%yr earlier
in the year. As with Adelaide and Brisbane, the detail shows

a more even spread of declines across price tiers. However,
turnover is showing a milder pull-back in Adelaide.
• The smaller capitals were again more mixed, price growth
still positive in Darwin (0.4%mth, 11.9%yr), relatively
resilient in Hobart (–0.5%mth, 7%yr) but weaker in
Canberra (–1.1%mth, –1.6%yr).
• Regional markets showed a 0.9% decline in Qld but slightly
milder declines in NSW (–0.7%) and Victoria (0.3%). Annual
prices growth is in the 3.4-6.3%yr range.

Our recently released Housing Pulse report (here) provides
a deeper diver into the situation in Australia's housing
markets. While the absence of selling pressure, still tight
supply-demand balance and sentiment mix suggests the
correction was still relatively 'orderly' through the September
quarter, the latest monthly data shows momentum remains
unambiguously negative.
That said, there are clearly more headwinds to come. The
RBA latest interest rate rise and what now looks to be a

more likely prospect of a follow-up increase at its meeting in
early November will impact in coming months. Next week's
consumer sentiment update will show how the change, and
the latest round of weak updates, is shaping housing-related
sentiment. Either way, momentum looks set to remain weak
into year-end and, barring a significantly improved inflation
and interest rate outlook, early 2027.

Following Nebius, AWS is
hiking EC2 capacity blocks

AWS 調漲  EC2 預留算⼒價格  15% ：鎖定  80 億美元輝達晶⽚交
易，公有雲定價權全⾯重構
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
OCT 7 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
Executive summary

即使最近我們觀察到部分 AI 伺服器產業鏈下游如 ODM 組裝廣達，滑軌川湖，
散熱系統雙鴻，及均熱⽚健策， 9 ⽉份訂單有明顯調整， 3Q26 營收⼩幅不如市
場預期，可能因為短期電⼒短缺及短料缺貨卡⻑料庫存調整，但 AI 數據算⼒中
⼼基礎建設的價格調整似乎還未停歇，先是有  Nebius 於 10/1 ⽇起全⾯調漲
AI GPU 租⽤價格達  17-21% ， CPU 達 25% ， Memory 達 41% （請參考之前
報告：17-21% On-demand AI GPU pricing hike to shift value from
LLM companies to cloud vendors），現在⼜有  AWS 於 10/7 ⽇起調漲  EC2
容量區塊牌價達 15% ，但使⽤客戶⼤語⾔模型公司  OpenAI, Anthropic 卻持
續降模型每百萬  Tokens 價格衝市佔，我們重申在電⼒短缺及短料缺貨卡⻑料
庫存造成既有算⼒稀缺的同時，短期市場價值將從賣  tokens 的⼤語⾔ / 專家

語⾔模型公司轉到  AI 數據中⼼算⼒基礎建設公司。

### Page 2

What’s new?
過去  24 ⼩時內，亞⾺遜雲端運算服務（ AWS ）官⽅更新了其  EC2 算⼒⽬
錄，正式⾃  2026 年  10 ⽉  7 ⽇起上調指定⾼階  GPU 預留容量區塊（ EC2
Capacity Blocks ）牌價，實質調漲幅度⾼達約  15% 。這項定價調整與亞⾺
遜正在推進的  80 億美元輝達  GPU 專項結構化融資案同步浮出⽔⾯。在此之
前，專⽤雲⿓頭  CoreWeave 與  Nebius 甫於  10 ⽉  1 ⽇將  H100 及  B200
現貨時租價格調漲  17% ⾄  21% 。 AWS 此次調價確認了公有雲⿓頭不再打價
格戰，⽽是全⾯跟進調價，彰顯出在企業⻑週期  Agent 與前線訓練負載湧⼊
下，頂級算⼒供給依舊供不應求。
付費訂閱戶可分享誰有利，誰受影響， Outside the

場。
Our outside the box non-consensus thinking
市場直覺認為雲端巨頭提價可能引發客戶流失或轉向⾃建資料中⼼。然⽽我們
的⾮共識洞察是  AWS 此次提價並⾮單純的成本轉嫁，⽽是算⼒期貨化與優質
客戶篩選機制優化。在  EC2 Capacity Blocks 機制下，客戶鎖定的是「未來
特定時段確保可⽤的確定性算⼒」。在全美資料中⼼正⾯臨超過  100 項地⽅性
建置禁令（ Moratoriums ）與電網審批塞⾞的背景下，「即時可⽤的電⼒與算
⼒」成為絕對稀缺品。 AWS 藉由調價  15% ，有效過濾了投機性短期負載，將

關鍵算⼒優先供給具備⻑期訂閱付費能⼒的⾼價值企業客戶（如⽣成式醫療、
⾼頻⾦融與國防客戶），從⽽鎖定⻑達  3 ⾄  5 年的現⾦流可預測性。
Our advises and risks analysis
超配雲端巨頭⺟公司股權：AWS 提價確認了強⼤的定價權，亞⾺遜
（ AMZN ）預計將在 2027-2028 展現⾼於預期的利潤率修復。
持有  GPU 虛擬化與排程優化軟體股：隨著時租成本攀升，企業將加⼤採
購動態排程與⼯作負載優化軟體，推升相關供應商營收。
客戶架構優化導致  Token 效率提升：推論優化演算法可能減少總  GPU
租賃時⻑，這對雲巨頭是漲價後可能發⽣的⾵險。
總體經濟逆⾵壓抑  IT ⽀出：若企業削減  2027 年  IT 資本預算，可能影
響⾼價預留容量的續約率。

附註： EC2 及 EC2 capacity blocks 是什麼 ?
Amazon EC2 （ Elastic Compute Cloud ）是亞⾺遜雲端運算服務（ AWS ）
最核⼼的  IaaS （基礎設施即服務）產品，簡單說就是「在雲端租⽤的虛擬伺
服器」。企業與開發者不再需要⾃⾏採購、架設或維護實體機房與伺服器硬

### Page 4

體，只要透過網路，幾分鐘內就能按需開啟或關閉任意運算規格的主機，並依
實際使⽤時間計費。
⼀、  EC2 的核⼼概念與結構
1. 實例（ Instances ）：
EC2 中的虛擬機器統稱為「實例」。
AWS 依據不同的⼯作負載，將實例分成不同家族，例如：
通⽤型（ M 系列、 T 系列）：平衡  CPU 、記憶體與網路，常⽤
於⼀般網站、後端  API 。
運算優化型（ C 系列）：⾼單核效能，適⽤於批次處理、分散式
分析。
記憶體優化型（ R 系列、 X 系列）：配備海量  RAM ，⽤於記憶
體資料庫（ Redis/SAP HANA ）。
加速運算型（ P 系列、 G 系列、 Trn 系列）：搭載  NVIDIA ⾼階
GPU （如  H100 、 B200 、 B300 等）或  AWS ⾃研晶⽚
（ Trainium/Inferentia ），專⾨⽤於  AI ⼤模型訓練與推論。
2. 彈性（ Elastic ）：
資源可以⽔平擴展（ Auto Scaling ）或垂直升降級。例如電商遇到

購物節流量爆發，系統可⾃動從  5 台擴增⾄  50 台，活動結束後⾃
動銷毀，避免資源閒置浪費。
⼆、  常⾒的計費與購買模式
傳統上， EC2 的採購模式主要分為：
隨需（ On-Demand ）：隨開隨⽤、隨關隨停，按秒 / ⼩時收費，價格最
⾼但彈性最⼤。
節約計畫  / 預留（ Savings Plans / Reserved Instances ）：承諾
使⽤  1 年或  3 年，換取⾼達  40%–70% 的折扣。
競價實例（ Spot Instances ）：利⽤  AWS 當下閒置的閒置運算容量，
折扣極⾼，但隨時可能被  AWS 回收中斷。
三、  什麼是「 EC2 Capacity Blocks 」？
在  AI 浪潮爆發後，⾼階  GPU 供不應求。許多  AI 新創或研究團隊買不起
1~3 年的⻑約（ Reserved ），但⽤隨需（ On-Demand ）⼜常因機房沒卡⽽
啟動失敗，⽤  Spot 則可能在模型訓練到⼀半時被踢掉。
因此  AWS 推出了  EC2 Capacity Blocks for ML （機器學習容量區塊）：

運作機制：允許客戶提前預約未來某個特定⽇期、特定時段（最⻑可預約
數週前，租期由  1 天⾄數⼗天不等）的頂級  GPU 算⼒叢集（如  P5 、 P6

### Page 5

考，並⾮投資建議，個⼈應審慎評估⾵險，如因此造成任何損失，本⼈不負任

A range of indicators suggests the dollar rally is becoming
stretched. But with no clear macro or market catalyst in sight,
we see little reason to call for an imminent reversal. We have
lowered our EUR forecasts to reﬂect a more durable ﬁscal risk
premium, while increasing prospects of a BoE hike should keep
EUR/GBP under pressure for longer

Francesco Pesole, Frantisek Taborsky and Chris Turner
Dollar strength continues to climb, supported by ﬁrmer oil prices
We have published the October edition of FX Talking: Au
revoir, euro bulls, with updated FX forecasts and views.
USD: Overbought signals not that
relevant for now
The start of the week has brought more dollar strength. Firmer oil
prices, following increased military activity in the Gulf, have helped
keep the dollar on solid footing. Today’s calendar is unlikely to be

particularly impactful, with the NFIB survey and home sales ﬁgures
